It is sent periodically, and if the ping is not acknowledged by the peer within a certain timeout period, the transport is disconnected.\\n4: \\n5: This guide documents the knobs within gRPC core to control the current behavior of the keepalive ping.\\n6: \\n7: The keepalive ping in core is controlled by the following channel arguments -\\n8: \\n9: * **GRPC_ARG_KEEPALIVE_TIME_MS**\\n10:   * This channel argument controls the period (in milliseconds) after which a keepalive ping is sent on the transport.\\n11: * **GRPC_ARG_KEEPALIVE_TIMEOUT_MS**\\n12:   * This channel argument controls the amount of time (in milliseconds) the sender of the keepalive ping waits for an acknowledgement. ### FAQ\\n42: * When is the keepalive timer started?\\n43:   * The keepalive timer is started when a transport is done connecting (after handshake).\\n44: * What happens when the keepalive timer fires?\\n45:   * When the keepalive timer fires, gRPC Core will try to send a keepalive ping on the transport. This ping can be blocked if -\\n46:     * there is no active call on that transport and `GRPC_ARG_KEEPALIVE_PERMIT_WITHOUT_CALLS` is false.\\n47:     * the number of pings already sent on the transport without any data has already exceeded `GRPC_ARG_HTTP2_MAX_PINGS_WITHOUT_DATA`.\\n48:   * If a keepalive ping is not blocked and is sent on the transport, then the keepalive watchdog timer is started which will close the transport if the ping is not acknowledged before it fires.\\n49: * Why am I receiving a GOAWAY with error code `ENHANCE_YOUR_CALM`?\\n50: ... It is sent periodically, and if the ping is not acknowledged by the peer within a certain timeout period, the transport is disconnected.\\n4: \\n5: This guide documents the knobs within gRPC core to control the current behavior of the keepalive ping.\\n6: \\n7: The keepalive ping in core is controlled by the following channel arguments -\\n8: \\n9: * **GRPC_ARG_KEEPALIVE_TIME_MS**\\n10:   * This channel argument controls the period (in milliseconds) after which a keepalive ping is sent on the transport.\\n11: * **GRPC_ARG_KEEPALIVE_TIMEOUT_MS**\\n12:   * This channel argument controls the amount of time (in milliseconds) the sender of the keepalive ping waits for an acknowledgement. ### FAQ\n42: * When is the keepalive timer started?\n43:   * The keepalive timer is started when a transport is done connecting (after handshake).\n44: * What happens when the keepalive timer fires?\n45:   * When the keepalive timer fires, gRPC Core will try to send a keepalive ping on the transport. This ping can be blocked if -\n46:     * there is no active call on that transport and `GRPC_ARG_KEEPALIVE_PERMIT_WITHOUT_CALLS` is false.\n47:     * the number of pings already sent on the transport without any data has already exceeded `GRPC_ARG_HTTP2_MAX_PINGS_WITHOUT_DATA`.\n48:   * If a keepalive ping is not blocked and is sent on the transport, then the keepalive watchdog timer is started which will close the transport if the ping is not acknowledged before it fires.\n49: * Why am I receiving a GOAWAY with error code `ENHANCE_YOUR_CALM`?\n50: ... It is sent periodically, and if the ping is not acknowledged by the peer within a certain timeout period, the transport is disconnected.\n4: \n5: This guide documents the knobs within gRPC core to control the current behavior of the keepalive ping.\n6: \n7: The keepalive ping in core is controlled by the following channel arguments -\n8: \n9: * **GRPC_ARG_KEEPALIVE_TIME_MS**\n10:   * This channel argument controls the period (in milliseconds) after which a keepalive ping is sent on the transport.\n11: * **GRPC_ARG_KEEPALIVE_TIMEOUT_MS**\n12:   * This channel argument controls the amount of time (in milliseconds) the sender of the keepalive ping waits for an acknowledgement. If it does not receive an acknowledgment within this time, it will close the connection.\n13: * **GRPC_ARG_HTTP2_MAX_PINGS_WITHOUT_DATA**\n14: ... ### FAQ\\n42: * When is the keepalive timer started?\\n43:   * The keepalive timer is started when a transport is done connecting (after handshake).\\n44: * What happens when the keepalive timer fires?\\n45:   * When the keepalive timer fires, gRPC Core will try to send a keepalive ping on the transport. This ping can be blocked if -\\n46:     * there is no active call on that transport and `GRPC_ARG_KEEPALIVE_PERMIT_WITHOUT_CALLS` is false.\\n47:     * the number of pings already sent on the transport without any data has already exceeded `GRPC_ARG_HTTP2_MAX_PINGS_WITHOUT_DATA`.\\n48:   * If a keepalive ping is not blocked and is sent on the transport, then the keepalive watchdog timer is started which will close the transport if the ping is not acknowledged before it fires.\\n49: * Why am I receiving a GOAWAY with error code `ENHANCE_YOUR_CALM`?\\n50: ... [chunk truncated]\",\"endLine\":57,\"startLine\":41},{\"content\":\"1: # Keepalive User Guide for gRPC Core (and dependents)\\n2: \\n3: The keepalive ping is a way to check if a channel is currently working by sending HTTP2 pings over the transport. It is sent periodically, and if the ping is not acknowledged by the peer within a certain timeout period, the transport is disconnected.\\n4: \\n5: This guide documents the knobs within gRPC core to control the current behavior of the keepalive ping.\\n6: \\n7: The keepalive ping in core is controlled by the following channel arguments -\\n8: \\n9: * **GRPC_ARG_KEEPALIVE_TIME_MS**\\n10:   * This channel argument controls the period (in milliseconds) after which a keepalive ping is sent on the transport.\\n11: * **GRPC_ARG_KEEPALIVE_TIMEOUT_MS**\\n12:   * This channel argument controls the amount of time (in milliseconds) the sender of the keepalive ping waits for an acknowledgement. If it does not receive an acknowledgment within this time, it will close the connection.\\n13: * **GRPC_ARG_HTTP2_MAX_PINGS_WITHOUT_DATA**\\n14: ... [chunk truncated]\",\"endLine\":30,\"startLine\":1},{\"content\":\"31: \\n32: Channel Argument| Client|Server\\n33: ----------------|-------|------\\n34: GRPC_ARG_KEEPALIVE_TIME_MS|INT_MAX ### FAQ\\n42: * When is the keepalive timer started?\\n43:   * The keepalive timer is started when a transport is done connecting (after handshake).\\n44: * What happens when the keepalive timer fires?\\n45:   * When the keepalive timer fires, gRPC Core will try to send a keepalive ping on the transport. This ping can be blocked if -\\n46:     * there is no active call on that transport and `GRPC_ARG_KEEPALIVE_PERMIT_WITHOUT_CALLS` is false.\\n47:     * the number of pings already sent on the transport without any data has already exceeded `GRPC_ARG_HTTP2_MAX_PINGS_WITHOUT_DATA`.\\n48:   * If a keepalive ping is not blocked and is sent on the transport, then the keepalive watchdog timer is started which will close the transport if the ping is not acknowledged before it fires.\\n49: * Why am I receiving a GOAWAY with error code `ENHANCE_YOUR_CALM`?\\n50: ... [chunk truncated]\",\"endLine\":57,\"startLine\":41},{\"content\":\"1: # Keepalive User Guide for gRPC Core (and dependents)\\n2: \\n3: The keepalive ping is a way to check if a channel is currently working by sending HTTP2 pings over the transport. It is sent periodically, and if the ping is not acknowledged by the peer within a certain timeout period, the transport is disconnected.\\n4: \\n5: This guide documents the knobs within gRPC core to control the current behavior of the keepalive ping.\\n6: \\n7: The keepalive ping in core is controlled by the following channel arguments -\\n8: \\n9: * **GRPC_ARG_KEEPALIVE_TIME_MS**\\n10:   * This channel argument controls the period (in milliseconds) after which a keepalive ping is sent on the transport.\\n11: * **GRPC_ARG_KEEPALIVE_TIMEOUT_MS**\\n12:   * This channel argument controls the amount of time (in milliseconds) the sender of the keepalive ping waits for an acknowledgement. If it does not receive an acknowledgment within this time, it will close the connection.\\n13: * **GRPC_ARG_HTTP2_MAX_PINGS_WITHOUT_DATA**\\n14: ... [chunk truncated]\",\"endLine\":30,\"startLine\":1},{\"content\":\"31: \\n32: Channel Argument| Client|Server\\n33: ----------------|-------|------\\n34: GRPC_ARG_KEEPALIVE_TIME_MS|INT_MAX ### FAQ\n42: * When is the keepalive timer started?\n43:   * The keepalive timer is started when a transport is done connecting (after handshake).\n44: * What happens when the keepalive timer fires?\n45:   * When the keepalive timer fires, gRPC Core will try to send a keepalive ping on the transport. This ping can be blocked if -\n46:     * there is no active call on that transport and `GRPC_ARG_KEEPALIVE_PERMIT_WITHOUT_CALLS` is false.\n47:     * the number of pings already sent on the transport without any data has already exceeded `GRPC_ARG_HTTP2_MAX_PINGS_WITHOUT_DATA`.\n48:   * If a keepalive ping is not blocked and is sent on the transport, then the keepalive watchdog timer is started which will close the transport if the ping is not acknowledged before it fires.\n49: * Why am I receiving a GOAWAY with error code `ENHANCE_YOUR_CALM`?\n50: ... [chunk truncated]","endLine":57,"startLine":41},{"content":"1: # Keepalive User Guide for gRPC Core (and dependents)\n2: \n3: The keepalive ping is a way to check if a channel is currently working by sending HTTP2 pings over the transport. It is sent periodically, and if the ping is not acknowledged by the peer within a certain timeout period, the transport is disconnected.\n4: \n5: This guide documents the knobs within gRPC core to control the current behavior of the keepalive ping.\n6: \n7: The keepalive ping in core is controlled by the following channel arguments -\n8: \n9: * **GRPC_ARG_KEEPALIVE_TIME_MS**\n10:   * This channel argument controls the period (in milliseconds) after which a keepalive ping is sent on the transport.\n11: * **GRPC_ARG_KEEPALIVE_TIMEOUT_MS**\n12:   * This channel argument controls the amount of time (in milliseconds) the sender of the keepalive ping waits for an acknowledgement. If it does not receive an acknowledgment within this time, it will close the connection.\n13: * **GRPC_ARG_HTTP2_MAX_PINGS_WITHOUT_DATA**\n14: ... [chunk truncated]","endLine":30,"startLine":1},{"content":"31: \n32: Channel Argument| Client|Server\n33: ----------------|-------|------\n34: GRPC_ARG_KEEPALIVE_TIME_MS|INT_MAX (disabled)|7200000 (2
